# Claim ledger: mm-games levels 1 to 3, concept audit round 2

Blind read of `l1` to `l13`, `review-1` to `review-3` and `homework-1` to `homework-3`, written before any earlier review, audit, ledger or author report was opened.
One row per learner-facing claim: prose, question, graded key, option text and `why`, hint, feedback line, explanation, summary line, model answer, rubric line and code solution.
Each row carries the basis of its screen or item: a short derivation, a named source, or the check in the round 2 concepts scripts (`docs/reports/mm-games-L<n>-concepts-r2.py`) that recomputes it.
Verdicts: `correct`, `wrong`, `imprecise`, `misleading`, `unverified`. Finding and note numbers refer to `docs/reviews/audit-mm-games-1-3-r2.md`.

Rows: 3207. Verdicts: correct 3177, wrong 1, imprecise 22, misleading 1, unverified 6.
